[tool result for toolu_015VKBL5zBiuqjcsiHJSYiQG]
1	# Verification Gap Review
2	
3	**Goal:** Find changed behavior that could break without reliable verification catching it. Ask one question — "if the behavior this change is supposed to produce broke where it's actually used, would verification fail?" Do not hunt for correctness bugs, but report genuine problems you notice while tracing verification.
4	
5	The main verification gap shapes are:
6	
7	1. **Regression gap:** the changed code regresses where it's used, and no test covering that use would fail.
8	2. **Missing-adoption gap:** a place that should now use the new behavior doesn't; it handles the same case its own way, or not at all, and no test would flag the omission.
9	3. **Broken-verification gap:** a test appears to cover the changed behavior, but would not actually protect it because it is skipped, flaky, not run in the normal verification path, or too weak to observe the regression.
10	
11	## Evidence Rules
12	
13	- Read a test before claiming what it covers, runs, asserts, or misses.
14	- Before claiming no test exists, search the whole repo by the symbol under test and by import references; expected file locations are not enough.
15	- Never assert what you did not verify. If a finding cannot be grounded, drop it.
16	- In a finding, say what you actually checked — "none of the tests I read cover this" — and show how far you looked. Say a test doesn't exist anywhere only when the symbol/import-reference search actually shows that.
17	- Do not assign severity, confidence, priority, or ranking.
18	
19	## Review Sequence
20	
21	### Step 1: Screen for behavioral change
22	
23	Screen each part of the change separately. If a part is non-behavioral, skip it. Call a part non-behavioral only when the changed code does not alter return values, thrown errors, caller-visible side effects, or observable state (including iteration order and emitted messages). Once a part meets that test, move on; do not inspect callers or tests for extra confirmation.
24	
25	Common non-behavioral examples: formatting, comments, whitespace; pure renames; trivial getters/setters and pass-throughs; type-only or compiler-enforced changes with no runtime effect; etc.
26	
27	Only outcomes produced by deterministic code are worth automatically testing; tests are useless on static source text and brittle on LLM output. Skip those parts.
28	
29	If every part is skipped, output the clean result (see Output Format).
30	
31	### Step 2: Find the behavior that changed
32	
33	Identify what behavior changed compared to the previous version: output, side effect, branch, error path, schema/event shape, config default, validation/authorization rule, external contract, etc. If the change affects more than one behavior, handle each separately.
34	
35	Treat broad-impact changes as behavioral even when no single changed line looks important: dependency, toolchain, build/config, data-file, etc.
36	
37	### Step 3: Trace where that behavior is used
38	
39	Trace the changed behavior to the places that observe it. Start with direct callers and registered entry points (routes, commands, DI), contract consumers (schemas, events, APIs, database readers), and reverse-dependency info if already available.
40	
41	Follow a path only while the changed behavior is reachable and unverified. Stop when a test at that boundary would fail, the consumer does not observe the changed behavior, or the next hop is guesswork (dynamic dispatch, reflection, outside-repo consumers, etc.). Prefer the nearest observable boundary, often one to three hops away, especially across contract, integration, or service edges. If there are more than five similar consumers, group obvious repeats and check representative paths; expand only when a consumer observes the behavior differently.
42	
43	### Step 4: Qualify the consumer, then check its test
44	
45	For each consumer, name the smallest realistic regression this consumer would observe: invert the branch, drop the default, omit the field, return the old error code, skip the integration call, etc. This is the Demonstration. If no such regression exists, drop the path; untested downstream code is not a finding.
46	
47	A `Missing-adoption gap` qualifies not by the adoption failure alone but by a supersession signal: the change gives clear evidence the new behavior is meant to replace the local one — PR intent, naming or docs, a replaced sibling site, deleted duplicate logic, or a test defining the new rule — and the local site shares the same observable contract. Without a supersession signal and a shared observable contract, it is a refactor suggestion, not a verification-gap finding. Once both hold, check whether any test for that site would flag the non-adoption; missing coverage of the non-adoption is the gap itself, not a disqualifier.
48	
49	Find and read the relevant test. Ask whether the Demonstration would make an assertion fail.
50	
51	- If yes, the behavior is verified. No finding.
52	- For a regression-style Demonstration: if no test runs the path, the test is skipped/flaky/not run normally, or the test runs the code without checking the changed result, report a `Regression gap` or `Broken-verification gap`.
53	- For a qualifying Missing-adoption case: if none of the site tests you found assert it adopts the new behavior, report a `Missing-adoption gap`.
54	
55	A test counts only if it runs normally and an assertion observes the changed output, branch, or contract. These do not count: no execution; source-text assertions that match a file's wording instead of running it; success/no-throw/snapshot-only checks; mock/log-call checks; human-only checks; tests that mock away the integration; e2e tests that pass through without checking the changed output; stale assertions or fixtures.
56	
57	For example, `expect(x ?? DEFAULT).toBe(DEFAULT)` passes when `x` is missing.
58	
59	Common patterns:
60	
61	- **Caller-path gap** — helper test covers the branch, but caller values skip it.
62	- **Contract drift** — payload/schema/event changes must be verified at the consumer.
63	- **Migration compatibility** — tests only create new-format rows or fresh schemas.
64	- **Phantom exception** — handled partial-failure path has no test.
65	- **Missing-adoption gap** — sibling site should use the new rule/helper and does not.
66	- **Removed verification** — deleted test or weakened assertion leaves behavior unpinned; removing a source-text assertion is not this, since it never counted.
67	
68	### Step 5: Confirm each finding is real
69	
70	Before writing a finding, re-open the specific tests or search results the finding relies on. Verify the Demonstration would not make any test you checked fail, or that the absence claim is backed by the symbol/import-reference search. Do not claim more than you verified; drop any finding you cannot ground.
71	
72	Explain why the test misses the bug using what the test sets up and checks.
73	
74	Do not report: compiler/type-checker-enforced cases; behavior already verified by an integration, contract, or e2e test; implementation-detail or mock-only tests; low coverage or a missing test file by itself; legacy untested code the change did not affect.
75	
76	Report genuine problems you noticed while tracing verification, even if they are not verification gaps. Put them under `Other findings` in the output. This permits reporting what you already reached, not extra hunting. A claim that code misbehaves is a defect, not a gap — it goes under `Other findings` for standard triage, however you found it.
77	
78	## OUTPUT FORMAT
79	
80	Emit each verification-gap finding as one block. No general advice, no severity or confidence. Triage trusts a gap finding as filed and does not re-verify it, so each block must stand on its own evidence.
81	
82	```markdown
83	### <one-line title naming the gap>
84	
85	- **Changed surface:** the exact behavior or contract that changed — `file:line`.
86	- **Impacted consumer or site:** named concretely with `file:line` (e.g. "the `createInvoice` mutation used by the billing dashboard at `billing/dashboard.ts:88`," not "callers of this function").
87	- **Existing test evidence:**
88	  - `Regression gap`: what the relevant test actually asserts, with `file:line`; or, if none, the symbol/import-reference searches run and their result.
89	  - `Missing-adoption gap`: tests for the impacted site, and whether any assert it adopts the new behavior.
90	  - `Broken-verification gap`: the apparent test or verification path, and why it does not count.
91	- **Missing verification:** the precise assertion or check that's absent.
92	- **Demonstration:**
93	  - `Regression gap` / `Broken-verification gap`: the concrete regression that would ship undetected, and why the tests you checked would not fail.
94	  - `Missing-adoption gap`: the case the site mishandles by not adopting the new behavior, and that none of the tests you read assert adoption.
95	- **Consequence:** the concrete thing that ships wrong — a regression the checked evidence would not catch, or a site that should use the new behavior and doesn't.
96	- **Disposition:** `patch` — name the test to add, fit to the repo's own way of verifying (don't impose a generic test pyramid) — or `defer` when the gap is real but not worth closing as part of this change, with one sentence of why.
97	```
98	
99	If you noticed genuine non-gap problems while tracing verification, append:
100	
101	```markdown
102	## Other findings
103	
104	- <description only; no severity, confidence, priority, or ranking>
105	```
106	
107	When you find no verification gaps and no other findings, output exactly this single line, not an empty response:
108	
109	`No verification gaps found.`
110	
111	## CONTENT SOURCE
112	
113	"Review content:" in the message that launched you gives the content itself or a path to read it from. Read the file when it is a path; either way that is the content under review, and this instruction file never is. If no content is supplied, or the file it points to is missing, empty, or unreadable, say exactly that and stop — never report a clean review for content you could not read.

66	+import { Link, Route, Routes, useParams } from 'react-router'
67	+import { useParks } from './data/useParks.js'
68	+
69	+const cardClass = 'rounded-[10px] border border-line bg-surface p-4'
70	+const linkClass = 'focus-ring rounded text-forest underline'
71	+
72	+function ParkListPlaceholder() {
73	+  return <h1 className="text-lg font-bold">All parks</h1>
74	+}
75	+
76	+function ParkDetailsPlaceholder({ parks }) {
77	+  const { id } = useParams()
78	+  const park = parks.find((p) => p.id === id)
79	+
80	+  if (!park) {
81	+    return (
82	+      <div>
83	+        <h1 className="text-lg font-bold">Park not found</h1>
84	+        <p className="mt-2">
85	+          <Link to="/" className={linkClass}>
86	+            Back to all parks
87	+          </Link>
88	+        </p>
89	+      </div>
90	+    )
91	+  }
92	+
93	+  return <h1 className="text-[28px] font-extrabold">{park.name}</h1>
94	+}
95	+
96	+function ParksPanel({ status, parks, retry }) {
97	+  if (status === 'loading') {
98	+    return (
99	+      <p role="status" className={cardClass}>
100	+        Loading parks…
101	+      </p>
102	+    )
103	+  }
104	+
105	+  if (status === 'error') {
106	+    return (
107	+      <div role="alert" className={cardClass}>
108	+        <p>Couldn't load parks. Try again.</p>
109	+        <button
110	+          type="button"
111	+          onClick={retry}
112	+          className="focus-ring mt-3 min-h-11 rounded-lg bg-forest px-4 font-semibold text-white hover:bg-forest-dark"
113	+        >
114	+          Retry
115	+        </button>
116	+      </div>
117	+    )
118	+  }
119	+
120	+  return (
121	+    <Routes>
122	+      <Route path="/" element={<ParkListPlaceholder />} />
123	+      <Route path="/parks/:id" element={<ParkDetailsPlaceholder parks={parks} />} />
124	+    </Routes>
125	+  )
126	+}
127	+
128	+export default function App() {
129	+  const { status, parks, retry } = useParks()
130	+
131	+  return (
132	+    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
133	+      <header className="bg-forest px-5 py-4 text-white">
134	+        <p className="text-xl font-extrabold">Find a Park</p>
135	+        <p className="text-sm">New York City area</p>
136	+        {status === 'ready' && <p className="text-sm">{parks.length} parks</p>}
137	+      </header>
138	+      <main className="flex flex-1 flex-col">
139	+        <aside aria-label="Parks" className="p-5">
140	+          <ParksPanel status={status} parks={parks} retry={retry} />
141	+        </aside>
142	+        <section aria-label="Map of parks" className="flex-1 p-5">
143	+          <p className="text-muted">Map coming soon.</p>
144	+        </section>
145	+      </main>
146	+    </div>
147	+  )
148	+}

321	diff --git a/src/data/parks.js b/src/data/parks.js
322	new file mode 100644
323	index 0000000..5bd3d0e
324	--- /dev/null
325	+++ b/src/data/parks.js
326	@@ -0,0 +1,91 @@
327	+// Null-safe park data layer. Normalization never invents values and never
328	+// mutates the parsed source: missing scalars become `null`, missing arrays `[]`.
329	+
330	+const imageModules = import.meta.glob('../assets/park-images/*.png', {
331	+  eager: true,
332	+  query: '?url',
333	+  import: 'default',
334	+})
335	+
336	+// filename stem (e.g. "prospect-1") -> bundled asset URL
337	+const bundledImages = Object.fromEntries(
338	+  Object.entries(imageModules).map(([path, url]) => [fileStem(path), url]),
339	+)
340	+
341	+function fileStem(pathOrUrl) {
342	+  const withoutQuery = pathOrUrl.split(/[?#]/)[0]
343	+  const file = withoutQuery.split('/').pop() ?? ''
344	+  const dot = file.lastIndexOf('.')
345	+  return dot > 0 ? file.slice(0, dot) : file
346	+}
347	+
348	+/** Map a source image URL to the bundled art with the same filename stem; unmatched URLs pass through unchanged. */
349	+export function resolveImageUrl(url) {
350	+  if (typeof url !== 'string') return url
351	+  return bundledImages[fileStem(url)] ?? url
352	+}
353	+
354	+function text(value) {
355	+  return typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() !== '' ? value : null
356	+}
357	+
358	+function finiteNumber(value) {
359	+  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null
360	+}
361	+
362	+function stringList(value) {
363	+  return Array.isArray(value) ? value.filter((item) => text(item) !== null) : []
364	+}
365	+
366	+export function normalizePark(raw) {
367	+  const park = raw && typeof raw === 'object' ? raw : {}
368	+  const location = park.location && typeof park.location === 'object' ? park.location : {}
369	+  const lat = finiteNumber(location.lat)
370	+  const lng = finiteNumber(location.lng)
371	+  const acreage = finiteNumber(park.acreage)
372	+  const rating = finiteNumber(park.rating)
373	+
374	+  return {
375	+    id: text(park.id),
376	+    name: text(park.name),
377	+    number: null,
378	+    description: text(park.description),
379	+    address: text(location.address),
380	+    lat,
381	+    lng,
382	+    hasCoords: lat !== null && lng !== null,
383	+    amenities: stringList(park.amenities),
384	+    hours: text(park.hours),
385	+    images: stringList(park.images).map(resolveImageUrl),
386	+    acreage: acreage !== null && acreage >= 0 ? acreage : null,
387	+    rating: rating !== null && rating >= 0 && rating <= 5 ? rating : null,
388	+  }
389	+}
390	+
391	+function byName(a, b) {
392	+  if (a.name === null) return b.name === null ? 0 : 1
393	+  if (b.name === null) return -1
394	+  return a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'en', { sensitivity: 'base' })
395	+}
396	+
397	+/** Normalize, sort A–Z by name and number 1–N. The number is assigned once here and shared by every view. */
398	+export function normalizeParks(rawParks) {
399	+  if (!Array.isArray(rawParks)) return []
400	+  return rawParks
401	+    .filter((raw) => raw && typeof raw === 'object')
402	+    .map(normalizePark)
403	+    .sort(byName)
404	+    .map((park, index) => ({ ...park, number: index + 1 }))
405	+}
406	+
407	+export async function loadParks() {
408	+  const response = await fetch(`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}data/parks.sample.json`)
409	+  if (!response.ok) {
410	+    throw new Error(`Failed to load parks: HTTP ${response.status}`)
411	+  }
412	+  const data = await response.json()
413	+  if (!Array.isArray(data)) {
414	+    throw new Error('Failed to load parks: expected an array')
415	+  }
416	+  return normalizeParks(data)
417	+}

433	+function deepFreeze(value) {
434	+  if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
435	+    Object.values(value).forEach(deepFreeze)
436	+    Object.freeze(value)
437	+  }
438	+  return value

467	+      hours: '6:00 AM - 1:00 AM',
468	+      acreage: 526,
469	+      rating: 4.7,

476	+    expect(highland.hasCoords).toBe(true)
477	+  })
478	+
479	+  it('keeps null and empty fields as null / [] without inventing defaults', () => {

482	+    const cedar = byId(parks, 'cedar-hill-nature-preserve')
483	+    expect(cedar.images).toEqual([])

486	+  })
487	+
488	+  it('does not mutate the parsed source', () => {
489	+    const source = structuredClone(sample)
490	+    const snapshot = structuredClone(source)
491	+    deepFreeze(source)
492	+    expect(() => normalizeParks(source)).not.toThrow()
493	+    expect(source).toEqual(snapshot)
494	+    expect(source[0].images[0]).toBe('https://images.example.com/prospect-1.jpg')
495	+  })
496	+
497	+  it('returns [] for a non-array input and skips non-object entries', () => {
498	+    expect(normalizeParks(null)).toEqual([])
499	+    expect(normalizeParks([null, 'x', { id: 'a', name: 'A' }])).toHaveLength(1)
500	+  })
501	+})

507	+    expect(park.images).toEqual([])
508	+  })
509	+
510	+  it('uses null for missing scalars and a missing location', () => {

514	+      lat: null,
515	+      lng: null,
516	+      hasCoords: false,

522	+

527	+    ['Infinity lng', { lat: 40.7, lng: Infinity }],
528	+    ['string coords', { lat: '40.7', lng: '-73.9' }],
529	+    ['null location', null],
530	+  ])('flags bad coordinates (%s) with hasCoords: false and keeps the park', (_label, location) => {
531	+    const parks = normalizeParks([{ id: 'x', name: 'X', location }])

533	+    expect(parks[0].hasCoords).toBe(false)

535	+
536	+  it('keeps rating null when it is not a finite number in 0–5', () => {

538	+    expect(normalizePark({ id: 'x', name: 'X', rating: -1 }).rating).toBeNull()
539	+    expect(normalizePark({ id: 'x', name: 'X', rating: '4' }).rating).toBeNull()
540	+    expect(normalizePark({ id: 'x', name: 'X', rating: 0 }).rating).toBe(0)
541	+  })

583	diff --git a/src/data/useParks.js b/src/data/useParks.js
584	new file mode 100644
585	index 0000000..4eefc8b
586	--- /dev/null
587	+++ b/src/data/useParks.js
588	@@ -0,0 +1,28 @@
589	+import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
590	+import { loadParks } from './parks.js'
591	+
592	+/** Loads parks once; `retry` refetches after an error. */
593	+export function useParks() {
594	+  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0)
595	+  const [state, setState] = useState({ status: 'loading', parks: [] })
596	+
597	+  useEffect(() => {
598	+    let cancelled = false
599	+    setState({ status: 'loading', parks: [] })
600	+    loadParks().then(
601	+      (parks) => {
602	+        if (!cancelled) setState({ status: 'ready', parks })
603	+      },
604	+      () => {
605	+        if (!cancelled) setState({ status: 'error', parks: [] })
606	+      },
607	+    )
608	+    return () => {
609	+      cancelled = true
610	+    }
611	+  }, [attempt])
612	+
613	+  const retry = useCallback(() => setAttempt((n) => n + 1), [])
614	+
615	+  return { status: state.status, parks: state.parks, retry }
616	+}

66	+import { Link, Route, Routes, useParams } from 'react-router'
67	+import { useParks } from './data/useParks.js'
68	+
69	+const cardClass = 'rounded-[10px] border border-line bg-surface p-4'
70	+const linkClass = 'focus-ring rounded text-forest underline'
71	+
72	+function ParkListPlaceholder() {
73	+  return <h1 className="text-lg font-bold">All parks</h1>
74	+}
75	+
76	+function ParkDetailsPlaceholder({ parks }) {
77	+  const { id } = useParams()
78	+  const park = parks.find((p) => p.id === id)
79	+
80	+  if (!park) {
81	+    return (
82	+      <div>
83	+        <h1 className="text-lg font-bold">Park not found</h1>
84	+        <p className="mt-2">
85	+          <Link to="/" className={linkClass}>
86	+            Back to all parks
87	+          </Link>
88	+        </p>
89	+      </div>
90	+    )
91	+  }
92	+
93	+  return <h1 className="text-[28px] font-extrabold">{park.name}</h1>
94	+}
95	+
96	+function ParksPanel({ status, parks, retry }) {
97	+  if (status === 'loading') {
98	+    return (
99	+      <p role="status" className={cardClass}>
100	+        Loading parks…
101	+      </p>
102	+    )
103	+  }
104	+
105	+  if (status === 'error') {
106	+    return (
107	+      <div role="alert" className={cardClass}>
108	+        <p>Couldn't load parks. Try again.</p>
109	+        <button
110	+          type="button"
111	+          onClick={retry}
112	+          className="focus-ring mt-3 min-h-11 rounded-lg bg-forest px-4 font-semibold text-white hover:bg-forest-dark"
113	+        >
114	+          Retry
115	+        </button>
116	+      </div>
117	+    )
118	+  }
119	+
120	+  return (
121	+    <Routes>
122	+      <Route path="/" element={<ParkListPlaceholder />} />
123	+      <Route path="/parks/:id" element={<ParkDetailsPlaceholder parks={parks} />} />
124	+    </Routes>
125	+  )
126	+}
127	+
128	+export default function App() {
129	+  const { status, parks, retry } = useParks()
130	+
131	+  return (
132	+    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
133	+      <header className="bg-forest px-5 py-4 text-white">
134	+        <p className="text-xl font-extrabold">Find a Park</p>

323	index 0000000..5bd3d0e

325	+++ b/src/data/parks.js
326	@@ -0,0 +1,91 @@
327	+// Null-safe park data layer. Normalization never invents values and never
328	+// mutates the parsed source: missing scalars become `null`, missing arrays `[]`.
329	+
330	+const imageModules = import.meta.glob('../assets/park-images/*.png', {
331	+  eager: true,
332	+  query: '?url',
333	+  import: 'default',
334	+})
335	+
336	+// filename stem (e.g. "prospect-1") -> bundled asset URL
337	+const bundledImages = Object.fromEntries(
338	+  Object.entries(imageModules).map(([path, url]) => [fileStem(path), url]),
339	+)
340	+
341	+function fileStem(pathOrUrl) {
342	+  const withoutQuery = pathOrUrl.split(/[?#]/)[0]
343	+  const file = withoutQuery.split('/').pop() ?? ''
344	+  const dot = file.lastIndexOf('.')
345	+  return dot > 0 ? file.slice(0, dot) : file
346	+}
347	+
348	+/** Map a source image URL to the bundled art with the same filename stem; unmatched URLs pass through unchanged. */
349	+export function resolveImageUrl(url) {
350	+  if (typeof url !== 'string') return url
351	+  return bundledImages[fileStem(url)] ?? url
352	+}
353	+
354	+function text(value) {
355	+  return typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() !== '' ? value : null
356	+}
357	+
358	+function finiteNumber(value) {
359	+  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null
360	+}
361	+
362	+function stringList(value) {
363	+  return Array.isArray(value) ? value.filter((item) => text(item) !== null) : []
364	+}
365	+
366	+export function normalizePark(raw) {
367	+  const park = raw && typeof raw === 'object' ? raw : {}
368	+  const location = park.location && typeof park.location === 'object' ? park.location : {}
369	+  const lat = finiteNumber(location.lat)
370	+  const lng = finiteNumber(location.lng)
371	+  const acreage = finiteNumber(park.acreage)
372	+  const rating = finiteNumber(park.rating)
373	+
374	+  return {
375	+    id: text(park.id),
376	+    name: text(park.name),
377	+    number: null,
378	+    description: text(park.description),
379	+    address: text(location.address),
380	+    lat,
381	+    lng,
382	+    hasCoords: lat !== null && lng !== null,
383	+    amenities: stringList(park.amenities),
384	+    hours: text(park.hours),
385	+    images: stringList(park.images).map(resolveImageUrl),
386	+    acreage: acreage !== null && acreage >= 0 ? acreage : null,
387	+    rating: rating !== null && rating >= 0 && rating <= 5 ? rating : null,
388	+  }
389	+}
390	+
391	+function byName(a, b) {
392	+  if (a.name === null) return b.name === null ? 0 : 1
393	+  if (b.name === null) return -1
394	+  return a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'en', { sensitivity: 'base' })
395	+}
396	+
397	+/** Normalize, sort A–Z by name and number 1–N. The number is assigned once here and shared by every view. */
398	+export function normalizeParks(rawParks) {
399	+  if (!Array.isArray(rawParks)) return []
400	+  return rawParks
401	+    .filter((raw) => raw && typeof raw === 'object')
402	+    .map(normalizePark)
403	+    .sort(byName)
404	+    .map((park, index) => ({ ...park, number: index + 1 }))
405	+}
406	+
407	+export async function loadParks() {
408	+  const response = await fetch(`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}data/parks.sample.json`)
409	+  if (!response.ok) {
410	+    throw new Error(`Failed to load parks: HTTP ${response.status}`)
411	+  }
412	+  const data = await response.json()
413	+  if (!Array.isArray(data)) {
414	+    throw new Error('Failed to load parks: expected an array')
415	+  }
416	+  return normalizeParks(data)
417	+}

429	+const sample = JSON.parse(
430	+  readFileSync(resolve(process.cwd(), 'public/data/parks.sample.json'), 'utf8'),

433	+function deepFreeze(value) {
434	+  if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
435	+    Object.values(value).forEach(deepFreeze)
436	+    Object.freeze(value)
437	+  }
438	+  return value
439	+}

477	+  })
478	+

494	+    expect(source[0].images[0]).toBe('https://images.example.com/prospect-1.jpg')
495	+  })
496	+
497	+  it('returns [] for a non-array input and skips non-object entries', () => {
498	+    expect(normalizeParks(null)).toEqual([])
499	+    expect(normalizeParks([null, 'x', { id: 'a', name: 'A' }])).toHaveLength(1)

583	diff --git a/src/data/useParks.js b/src/data/useParks.js
584	new file mode 100644
585	index 0000000..4eefc8b
586	--- /dev/null
587	+++ b/src/data/useParks.js
588	@@ -0,0 +1,28 @@
589	+import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
590	+import { loadParks } from './parks.js'
591	+
592	+/** Loads parks once; `retry` refetches after an error. */
593	+export function useParks() {
594	+  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0)
595	+  const [state, setState] = useState({ status: 'loading', parks: [] })
596	+
597	+  useEffect(() => {
598	+    let cancelled = false
599	+    setState({ status: 'loading', parks: [] })
600	+    loadParks().then(
601	+      (parks) => {
602	+        if (!cancelled) setState({ status: 'ready', parks })
603	+      },
604	+      () => {
605	+        if (!cancelled) setState({ status: 'error', parks: [] })
606	+      },
607	+    )
608	+    return () => {
609	+      cancelled = true
610	+    }
611	+  }, [attempt])
612	+
613	+  const retry = useCallback(() => setAttempt((n) => n + 1), [])
614	+
615	+  return { status: state.status, parks: state.parks, retry }
616	+}

<frozen-after-approval reason="human-owned intent — do not modify unless human renegotiates">

## Intent

**Problem:** There is no app yet. Every later story needs a runnable Vite + React + Tailwind project, park data that cannot crash the UI, routes, a layout shell, and bundled park images.

**Approach:** Scaffold the app and fetch `parks.sample.json` from `public/` through a null-safe loader. The loader normalizes the data, sorts parks A–Z, numbers them 1–N and maps image URLs to bundled art. Wrap it in a loading/error/retry hook, and render landmark regions with placeholder content for routes `/` and `/parks/:id`.

## Boundaries & Constraints

**Always:** one `npm install` + `npm run dev`; no API keys; `requirements/parks.sample.json` copied byte-for-byte and never edited; normalization never fabricates values and never mutates the parsed source; nested reads null-safe; tokens, the Public Sans font and the amber focus ring come from `design/README.md`.

**Never:** list cards, details content, a map, or responsive layout work (Stories 1.2–1.5); geolocation; a component library; a static JSON import (it cannot fail, so the error state would be fiction; this decision came from the readiness gate).

## I/O & Edge-Case Matrix

| Scenario | Input / State | Expected Output / Behavior | Error Handling |
|----------|--------------|---------------------------|----------------|
| Happy path | 12 sample parks | 12 parks sorted A–Z by name, `number` 1–12 | N/A |
| Missing nested field | Highland: no `location.address` | `address: null`, no throw | N/A |
| Null / empty fields | Old Mill `description: null`; Cedar Hill `images: []`, `rating: null` | stay `null` / `[]`; no defaults invented | N/A |
| Missing arrays | `amenities` or `images` absent | `[]` | N/A |
| Bad coordinates | lat/lng missing or non-finite | `hasCoords: false`; park kept | N/A |
| Image mapped | `https://images.example.com/prospect-1.jpg` | bundled `prospect-1.png` URL | N/A |
| Image unmatched | `https://x.test/zzz.jpg` | URL unchanged | `onError` fallback later (1.3) |
| Load fails | fetch rejects or returns non-OK | "Couldn't load parks. Try again." + Retry button | Retry refetches |
| Loading | fetch pending | "Loading parks…" | N/A |
| Unknown route id | `/parks/nope` | "Park not found" + link back to `/` | N/A |

</frozen-after-approval>

## Code Map

- Greenfield repo: only `README.md`, `.gitignore` (already ignores `node_modules`, `dist`; `public` is not ignored), `design/`, `requirements/`, and transcripts. Do not touch `design/screens`, `design/source`, the transcripts or `_bmad*`.
- `requirements/parks.sample.json` -- source data; copy it to `public/data/parks.sample.json`.
- `design/park-images/*.png` (12 files) -- `git mv` them to `src/assets/park-images/`; the stems match all 12 sample URLs.
- `design/README.md` -- tokens, font, focus-ring classes, landmark names, loading/error copy.
- Toolchain: Node 25, npm 11. Current releases: vite 8, @vitejs/plugin-react 6, tailwindcss + @tailwindcss/vite 4 (CSS `@theme`, no config file), react 19, react-router 8, vitest 5.

## Tasks & Acceptance

**Execution:**
- [x] `package.json`, `vite.config.js`, `index.html` -- Vite + React + `@tailwindcss/vite`; scripts `dev`, `build`, `test` (`vitest run`); add the Vitest jsdom config and RTL setup; load Public Sans 400–800 from Google Fonts in `index.html`; set the title to "Find a Park".
- [x] `src/index.css` -- `@import "tailwindcss"`; put the `@theme` colour tokens and font from `design/README.md` here and define a `focus-ring` utility with the amber focus-visible outline.
- [x] `public/data/parks.sample.json` -- byte copy of the requirements file.
- [x] `src/assets/park-images/` -- moved pixel art.
- [x] `src/data/parks.js` -- `normalizePark`, `normalizeParks` (sort A–Z, assign `number`), `resolveImageUrl` (stem lookup via `import.meta.glob(..., { eager: true, query: '?url', import: 'default' })`), `loadParks()` (fetch `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}data/parks.sample.json`, throw on non-OK). Normalized shape: `{ id, name, number, description, address, lat, lng, hasCoords, amenities, hours, images, acreage, rating }`. Use `null` for missing scalars and `[]` for missing arrays.
- [x] `src/data/useParks.js` -- hook returning `{ status: 'loading'|'error'|'ready', parks, retry }`.
- [x] `src/App.jsx`, `src/main.jsx` -- `BrowserRouter`; routes `/` and `/parks/:id`. The shell renders `<header>` (app name, park count once ready), `<main>`, `<aside aria-label="Parks">` (placeholder: the "All parks" heading on `/`; the park name, or "Park not found" with a link back, on `/parks/:id`) and `<section aria-label="Map of parks">` (placeholder). Loading and error states render inside the aside.
- [x] `src/data/parks.test.js`, `src/App.test.jsx` -- unit-test every I/O matrix row, including that the source object is not mutated; test the loading → error → retry → ready flow by mocking `fetch`.

**Acceptance Criteria:**
- Given a fresh clone, when `npm install` and `npm run dev` run, then the app serves locally with no keys.
- Given the app at `/parks/prospect-park`, when the user presses browser Back after navigating from `/`, then `/` renders again.
- Given `public/data/parks.sample.json`, when compared with `requirements/parks.sample.json`, then the bytes are identical.

## Implementation Notes

- vitest 4.1 + jsdom 29 instead of vitest 5 + jsdom 30: the latter declare engines that exclude Node 25 (odd release), so plain `npm install` on Node 25 picks v4/v29 and installs warning-free. Upgrade when on Node 24 LTS or 26.
- Park PNGs are under Vite's 4 KB inline limit, so `?url` yields data URIs in the production build (bundled, no separate files).
- Tests read the fixture with `process.cwd()` because jsdom gives `import.meta.url` an http scheme.
- Normalization also treats empty/whitespace strings as `null` and out-of-range `acreage` (< 0) as `null`; non-object entries are skipped.

## Spec Change Log

## Review Triage Log

## Design Notes

The park `number` is assigned once at load time and shared by list cards and map markers (Stories 1.2 and 1.4), so it must not be recomputed per view. `rating` stays `null` when it is not a finite number in 0–5, so the UI can never show 0 stars by accident.

## Verification

**Commands:**
- `npm test` -- expected: all tests pass.
- `npm run build` -- expected: succeeds with no errors.
- `cmp requirements/parks.sample.json public/data/parks.sample.json` -- expected: no output.

**Manual checks (if no CLI):**
- `npm run dev`, then open `/` and `/parks/nope`: the landmarks are present, "Park not found" shows with its link, and Tab shows the amber focus ring on the link.

## I/O & Edge-Case Matrix

| Scenario | Input / State | Expected Output / Behavior | Error Handling |
|----------|--------------|---------------------------|----------------|
| Happy path | 12 sample parks | 12 parks sorted A–Z by name, `number` 1–12 | N/A |
| Missing nested field | Highland: no `location.address` | `address: null`, no throw | N/A |
| Null / empty fields | Old Mill `description: null`; Cedar Hill `images: []`, `rating: null` | stay `null` / `[]`; no defaults invented | N/A |
| Missing arrays | `amenities` or `images` absent | `[]` | N/A |
| Bad coordinates | lat/lng missing or non-finite | `hasCoords: false`; park kept | N/A |
| Image mapped | `https://images.example.com/prospect-1.jpg` | bundled `prospect-1.png` URL | N/A |
| Image unmatched | `https://x.test/zzz.jpg` | URL unchanged | `onError` fallback later (1.3) |
| Load fails | fetch rejects or returns non-OK | "Couldn't load parks. Try again." + Retry button | Retry refetches |
| Loading | fetch pending | "Loading parks…" | N/A |
| Unknown route id | `/parks/nope` | "Park not found" + link back to `/` | N/A |

</frozen-after-approval>

## Code Map
## Implementation Notes

- vitest 4.1 + jsdom 29 instead of vitest 5 + jsdom 30: the latter declare engines that exclude Node 25 (odd release), so plain `npm install` on Node 25 picks v4/v29 and installs warning-free. Upgrade when on Node 24 LTS or 26.
- Park PNGs are under Vite's 4 KB inline limit, so `?url` yields data URIs in the production build (bundled, no separate files).
- Tests read the fixture with `process.cwd()` because jsdom gives `import.meta.url` an http scheme.
- Normalization also treats empty/whitespace strings as `null` and out-of-range `acreage` (< 0) as `null`; non-object entries are skipped.

16	## Intent
17	
18	**Problem:** There is no app yet. Every later story needs a runnable Vite + React + Tailwind project, park data that cannot crash the UI, routes, a layout shell, and bundled park images.
19	
20	**Approach:** Scaffold the app and fetch `parks.sample.json` from `public/` through a null-safe loader. The loader normalizes the data, sorts parks A–Z, numbers them 1–N and maps image URLs to bundled art. Wrap it in a loading/error/retry hook, and render landmark regions with placeholder content for routes `/` and `/parks/:id`.
21	
22	## Boundaries & Constraints
23	
24	**Always:** one `npm install` + `npm run dev`; no API keys; `requirements/parks.sample.json` copied byte-for-byte and never edited; normalization never fabricates values and never mutates the parsed source; nested reads null-safe; tokens, the Public Sans font and the amber focus ring come from `design/README.md`.
25	
26	**Never:** list cards, details content, a map, or responsive layout work (Stories 1.2–1.5); geolocation; a component library; a static JSON import (it cannot fail, so the error state would be fiction; this decision came from the readiness gate).
27	
28	## I/O & Edge-Case Matrix
29	
30	| Scenario | Input / State | Expected Output / Behavior | Error Handling |

32	| Happy path | 12 sample parks | 12 parks sorted A–Z by name, `number` 1–12 | N/A |
33	| Missing nested field | Highland: no `location.address` | `address: null`, no throw | N/A |
34	| Null / empty fields | Old Mill `description: null`; Cedar Hill `images: []`, `rating: null` | stay `null` / `[]`; no defaults invented | N/A |
35	| Missing arrays | `amenities` or `images` absent | `[]` | N/A |
36	| Bad coordinates | lat/lng missing or non-finite | `hasCoords: false`; park kept | N/A |
37	| Image mapped | `https://images.example.com/prospect-1.jpg` | bundled `prospect-1.png` URL | N/A |
38	| Image unmatched | `https://x.test/zzz.jpg` | URL unchanged | `onError` fallback later (1.3) |

40	| Loading | fetch pending | "Loading parks…" | N/A |
41	| Unknown route id | `/parks/nope` | "Park not found" + link back to `/` | N/A |

45	## Code Map
46	
47	- Greenfield repo: only `README.md`, `.gitignore` (already ignores `node_modules`, `dist`; `public` is not ignored), `design/`, `requirements/`, and transcripts. Do not touch `design/screens`, `design/source`, the transcripts or `_bmad*`.
48	- `requirements/parks.sample.json` -- source data; copy it to `public/data/parks.sample.json`.
49	- `design/park-images/*.png` (12 files) -- `git mv` them to `src/assets/park-images/`; the stems match all 12 sample URLs.
50	- `design/README.md` -- tokens, font, focus-ring classes, landmark names, loading/error copy.
51	- Toolchain: Node 25, npm 11. Current releases: vite 8, @vitejs/plugin-react 6, tailwindcss + @tailwindcss/vite 4 (CSS `@theme`, no config file), react 19, react-router 8, vitest 5.

53	## Tasks & Acceptance
54	
55	**Execution:**
56	- [x] `package.json`, `vite.config.js`, `index.html` -- Vite + React + `@tailwindcss/vite`; scripts `dev`, `build`, `test` (`vitest run`); add the Vitest jsdom config and RTL setup; load Public Sans 400–800 from Google Fonts in `index.html`; set the title to "Find a Park".
57	- [x] `src/index.css` -- `@import "tailwindcss"`; put the `@theme` colour tokens and font from `design/README.md` here and define a `focus-ring` utility with the amber focus-visible outline.
58	- [x] `public/data/parks.sample.json` -- byte copy of the requirements file.
59	- [x] `src/assets/park-images/` -- moved pixel art.
60	- [x] `src/data/parks.js` -- `normalizePark`, `normalizeParks` (sort A–Z, assign `number`), `resolveImageUrl` (stem lookup via `import.meta.glob(..., { eager: true, query: '?url', import: 'default' })`), `loadParks()` (fetch `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}data/parks.sample.json`, throw on non-OK). Normalized shape: `{ id, name, number, description, address, lat, lng, hasCoords, amenities, hours, images, acreage, rating }`. Use `null` for missing scalars and `[]` for missing arrays.
61	- [x] `src/data/useParks.js` -- hook returning `{ status: 'loading'|'error'|'ready', parks, retry }`.
62	- [x] `src/App.jsx`, `src/main.jsx` -- `BrowserRouter`; routes `/` and `/parks/:id`. The shell renders `<header>` (app name, park count once ready), `<main>`, `<aside aria-label="Parks">` (placeholder: the "All parks" heading on `/`; the park name, or "Park not found" with a link back, on `/parks/:id`) and `<section aria-label="Map of parks">` (placeholder). Loading and error states render inside the aside.
63	- [x] `src/data/parks.test.js`, `src/App.test.jsx` -- unit-test every I/O matrix row, including that the source object is not mutated; test the loading → error → retry → ready flow by mocking `fetch`.
64

66	- Given a fresh clone, when `npm install` and `npm run dev` run, then the app serves locally with no keys.
67	- Given the app at `/parks/prospect-park`, when the user presses browser Back after navigating from `/`, then `/` renders again.
68	- Given `public/data/parks.sample.json`, when compared with `requirements/parks.sample.json`, then the bytes are identical.

70	## Implementation Notes
71	
72	- vitest 4.1 + jsdom 29 instead of vitest 5 + jsdom 30: the latter declare engines that exclude Node 25 (odd release), so plain `npm install` on Node 25 picks v4/v29 and installs warning-free. Upgrade when on Node 24 LTS or 26.
73	- Park PNGs are under Vite's 4 KB inline limit, so `?url` yields data URIs in the production build (bundled, no separate files).
74	- Tests read the fixture with `process.cwd()` because jsdom gives `import.meta.url` an http scheme.
75	- Normalization also treats empty/whitespace strings as `null` and out-of-range `acreage` (< 0) as `null`; non-object entries are skipped.

81	## Design Notes
82	
83	The park `number` is assigned once at load time and shared by list cards and map markers (Stories 1.2 and 1.4), so it must not be recomputed per view. `rating` stays `null` when it is not a finite number in 0–5, so the UI can never show 0 stars by accident.
84

93	- `npm run dev`, then open `/` and `/parks/nope`: the landmarks are present, "Park not found" shows with its link, and Tab shows the amber focus ring on the link.

Two parks with equal names (case- and accent-insensitive) are numbered in whatever order the source file lists them. Two parks with the same `id` make `parks.find` always return the first. Neither case is flagged, skipped or tested. 70:## Implementation Notes
71-
72-- vitest 4.1 + jsdom 29 instead of vitest 5 + jsdom 30: the latter declare engines that exclude Node 25 (odd release), so plain `npm install` on Node 25 picks v4/v29 and installs warning-free. Upgrade when on Node 24 LTS or 26.
73-- Park PNGs are under Vite's 4 KB inline limit, so `?url` yields data URIs in the production build (bundled, no separate files).
74-- Tests read the fixture with `process.cwd()` because jsdom gives `import.meta.url` an http scheme.
75-- Normalization also treats empty/whitespace strings as `null` and out-of-range `acreage` (< 0) as `null`; non-object entries are skipped.
76-
77-## Spec Change Log
78-
79-## Review Triage Log
80-
81-## Design Notes
82-
83-The park `number` is assigned once at load time and shared by list cards and map markers (Stories 1.2 and 1.4), so it must not be recomputed per view. `rating` stays `null` when it is not a finite number in 0–5, so the UI can never show 0 stars by accident.
84-

+import { Link, Route, Routes, useParams } from 'react-router'
+import { useParks } from './data/useParks.js'
+
+const cardClass = 'rounded-[10px] border border-line bg-surface p-4'
+const linkClass = 'focus-ring rounded text-forest underline'
+
+function ParkListPlaceholder() {
+  return <h1 className="text-lg font-bold">All parks</h1>
+}
+
+function NotFound() {
+  return (
+    <div>
+      <h1 className="text-lg font-bold">Park not found</h1>
+      <p className="mt-2">
+        <Link to="/" className={linkClass}>
+          Back to all parks
+        </Link>
+      </p>
+    </div>
+  )
+}
+
+function ParkDetailsPlaceholder({ parks }) {
+  const { id } = useParams()
+  const park = parks.find((p) => p.id === id)
+
+  if (!park) return <NotFound />
+
+  return <h1 className="text-[28px] font-extrabold">{park.name}</h1>
+}
+
+function ParksPanel({ status, parks, retry }) {
+  if (status === 'loading') {
+    return (
+      <p role="status" className={cardClass}>
+        Loading parks…
+      </p>
+    )
+  }
+
+  if (status === 'error') {
+    return (
+      <div role="alert" className={cardClass}>
+        <p>Couldn't load parks. Try again.</p>
+        <button
+          type="button"
+          onClick={retry}
+          className="focus-ring mt-3 min-h-11 rounded-lg bg-forest px-4 font-semibold text-white hover:bg-forest-dark"
+        >
+          Retry
+        </button>
+      </div>
+    )
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <Routes>
+      <Route path="/" element={<ParkListPlaceholder />} />
+      <Route path="/parks/:id" element={<ParkDetailsPlaceholder parks={parks} />} />
+      <Route path="*" element={<NotFound />} />
+    </Routes>
+  )
+}
+
+export default function App() {
+  const { status, parks, retry } = useParks()
+
+  return (
+    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
+      <header className="bg-forest px-5 py-4 text-white">
+        <p className="text-xl font-extrabold">Find a Park</p>
+        <p className="text-sm">New York City area</p>
+        {status === 'ready' && <p className="text-sm">{parks.length} parks</p>}
+      </header>
+      <main className="flex flex-1 flex-col">
+        <aside aria-label="Parks" className="p-5">
+          <ParksPanel status={status} parks={parks} retry={retry} />
+        </aside>
+        <section aria-label="Map of parks" className="flex-1 p-5">
+          <p className="text-muted">Map coming soon.</p>
+        </section>
+      </main>
+    </div>
+  )
+}

+// Null-safe park data layer. Normalization never invents values and never
+// mutates the parsed source: missing scalars become `null`, missing arrays `[]`.
+
+const imageModules = import.meta.glob('../assets/park-images/*.png', {
+  eager: true,
+  query: '?url',
+  import: 'default',
+})
+
+// filename stem (e.g. "prospect-1") -> bundled asset URL
+const bundledImages = Object.fromEntries(
+  Object.entries(imageModules).map(([path, url]) => [fileStem(path), url]),
+)
+
+function fileStem(pathOrUrl) {
+  const withoutQuery = pathOrUrl.split(/[?#]/)[0]
+  const file = withoutQuery.split('/').pop() ?? ''
+  const dot = file.lastIndexOf('.')
+  return dot > 0 ? file.slice(0, dot) : file
+}
+

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
merge_session_transcripts.py

Collect the main transcript and every subagent transcript for a Claude Code
session ID, then interleave them into one timeline sorted by timestamp.

Claude Code stores sessions under ~/.claude/projects/<project-slug>/:
    <session-id>.jsonl                       main conversation
    <session-id>/subagents/agent-*.jsonl     each subagent's full transcript
    <session-id>/**/*.jsonl                  any other agent transcripts (picked up too)

Usage:
    python3 merge_session_transcripts.py <session-id>
    python3 merge_session_transcripts.py <session-id> -f md -o session.md
    python3 merge_session_transcripts.py <session-id> -f jsonl -o merged.jsonl
    python3 merge_session_transcripts.py <session-id> --projects-dir /path/to/.claude/projects

Formats:
    text   readable plain text (default)
    md     Markdown, one heading per message
    jsonl  the original JSON entries, merged, each tagged with "_source"
"""

import argparse
import json
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

DEFAULT_PROJECTS_DIR = Path.home() / ".claude" / "projects"
MIN_TS = datetime.min.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- discovery

def find_session_files(session_id: str, projects_dir: Path):
    """Return (main_file or None, [agent files]) for the session ID."""
    mains = sorted(projects_dir.glob(f"*/{session_id}.jsonl"))
    session_dirs = sorted(p for p in projects_dir.glob(f"*/{session_id}") if p.is_dir())

    if not mains and not session_dirs:
        return None, []

    if len(mains) > 1:
        print(f"warning: session found in {len(mains)} project folders; using all of them",
              file=sys.stderr)

    agent_files = []
    for d in session_dirs:
        agent_files.extend(sorted(d.rglob("*.jsonl")))

    return (mains, agent_files)


def source_label(path: Path, session_id: str) -> str:
    if path.name == f"{session_id}.jsonl":
        return "main"
    stem = path.stem  # e.g. agent-a1b2c3
    meta = path.with_suffix(".meta.json")
    if meta.exists():
        try:
            m = json.loads(meta.read_text())
            kind = m.get("agentType") or m.get("subagent_type") or m.get("type")
            if kind:
                return f"{stem} ({kind})"
        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
            pass
    return stem


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- loading

def parse_ts(value):
    if not value or not isinstance(value, str):
        return None
    try:
        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
        return dt if dt.tzinfo else dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    except ValueError:
        return None


def load_entries(path: Path, label: str, file_index: int):
    """Read one JSONL file. Entries with no timestamp borrow their neighbor's,
    so metadata lines stay next to the messages they belong with."""
    raw = []
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        for line_no, line in enumerate(fh):
            line = line.strip()
            if not line:
                continue
            try:
                entry = json.loads(line)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                print(f"warning: skipping bad JSON at {path}:{line_no + 1}", file=sys.stderr)
                continue
            raw.append((line_no, entry, parse_ts(entry.get("timestamp"))))

    # carry the last known timestamp forward; backfill leading gaps with the first one
    first_ts = next((ts for _, _, ts in raw if ts), None)
    last = first_ts or MIN_TS
    out = []
    for line_no, entry, ts in raw:
        if ts:
            last = ts
        out.append({
            "ts": ts or last,
            "has_ts": ts is not None,
            "file_index": file_index,
            "line_no": line_no,
            "source": label,
            "entry": entry,
        })
    return out


def merge(session_id: str, projects_dir: Path):
    mains, agent_files = find_session_files(session_id, projects_dir)
    if mains is None:
        sys.exit(f"error: no session '{session_id}' under {projects_dir}")

    files = list(mains) + agent_files
    records, seen_uuids = [], set()
    # read agent files first so a duplicated sidechain turn keeps its agent label;
    # file_index still reflects main-first order for tie-breaking in the sort
    order = list(range(len(mains), len(files))) + list(range(len(mains)))
    for i in order:
        path = files[i]
        label = source_label(path, session_id)
        for rec in load_entries(path, label, i):
            uuid = rec["entry"].get("uuid")
            # older versions also wrote subagent ("sidechain") turns inline in the
            # main file; drop exact duplicates by uuid so nothing appears twice
            if uuid:
                if uuid in seen_uuids:
                    continue
                seen_uuids.add(uuid)
            records.append(rec)

    # stable sort: timestamp, then file, then original line order
    records.sort(key=lambda r: (r["ts"], r["file_index"], r["line_no"]))
    return files, records


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- rendering

def block_to_text(block, max_tool_chars):
    if isinstance(block, str):
        return block
    if not isinstance(block, dict):
        return str(block)
    kind = block.get("type")
    if kind == "text":
        return block.get("text", "")
    if kind == "thinking":
        return f"[thinking]\n{block.get('thinking', '')}"
    if kind == "redacted_thinking":
        return "[redacted thinking]"
    if kind == "tool_use":
        args = json.dumps(block.get("input", {}), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        return f"[tool call: {block.get('name')} id={block.get('id')}]\n{clip(args, max_tool_chars)}"
    if kind == "tool_result":
        content = block.get("content")
        if isinstance(content, list):
            content = "\n".join(block_to_text(b, max_tool_chars) for b in content)
        status = " ERROR" if block.get("is_error") else ""
        return f"[tool result{status} for {block.get('tool_use_id')}]\n{clip(str(content or ''), max_tool_chars)}"
    if kind == "image":
        return "[image]"
    return f"[{kind} block]"


def clip(text, limit):
    if limit and len(text) > limit:
        return text[:limit] + f"\n... [{len(text) - limit} more chars]"
    return text


def entry_body(entry, max_tool_chars):
    msg = entry.get("message")
    if isinstance(msg, dict):
        content = msg.get("content")
        if isinstance(content, list):
            return "\n\n".join(block_to_text(b, max_tool_chars) for b in content)
        return str(content or "")
    for key in ("summary", "content", "text"):
        if isinstance(entry.get(key), str):
            return entry[key]
    return ""


def entry_role(entry):
    msg = entry.get("message")
    role = msg.get("role") if isinstance(msg, dict) else None
    return role or entry.get("type") or "entry"


def render_text(records, md, include_meta, max_tool_chars):
    lines = []
    for r in records:
        e = r["entry"]
        if e.get("type") not in ("user", "assistant") and not include_meta:
            continue
        body = entry_body(e, max_tool_chars).strip()
        if not body and not include_meta:
            continue
        ts = r["ts"].isoformat() if r["ts"] != MIN_TS else "no timestamp"
        if not r["has_ts"]:
            ts += " (inferred)"
        header = f"{ts}  [{r['source']}]  {entry_role(e)}"
        if md:
            lines.append(f"### {header}\n\n```\n{body}\n```\n" if body else f"### {header}\n")
        else:
            lines.append(f"===== {header} =====\n{body}\n")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def render_jsonl(records):
    out = []
    for r in records:
        e = dict(r["entry"])
        e["_source"] = r["source"]
        if not r["has_ts"]:
            e["_inferred_timestamp"] = r["ts"].isoformat() if r["ts"] != MIN_TS else None
        out.append(json.dumps(e, ensure_ascii=False))
    return "\n".join(out) + "\n"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- main

def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("session_id")
    ap.add_argument("-f", "--format", choices=["text", "md", "jsonl"], default="text")
    ap.add_argument("-o", "--output", help="write here instead of stdout")
    ap.add_argument("--projects-dir", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_PROJECTS_DIR)
    ap.add_argument("--include-meta", action="store_true",
                    help="also show non-message entries (summaries, snapshots, system lines)")
    ap.add_argument("--max-tool-chars", type=int, default=4000,
                    help="truncate tool inputs/results in text/md output (0 = no limit)")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    files, records = merge(args.session_id, args.projects_dir.expanduser())

    if args.format == "jsonl":
        result = render_jsonl(records)
    else:
        result = render_text(records, args.format == "md", args.include_meta, args.max_tool_chars)

    if args.output:
        Path(args.output).write_text(result, encoding="utf-8")
    else:
        sys.stdout.write(result)

    agents = len(files) - sum(1 for f in files if f.name == f"{args.session_id}.jsonl")
    print(f"merged {len(records)} entries from {len(files)} file(s) "
          f"({agents} agent transcript(s))", file=sys.stderr)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
